# Annexure B: register of every test that ran

Generated by `scripts/report/test_register.py` from the evidence campaign at commit `31eb935` and the gate's log. One row per test a runner reported; the same rows are in `data/test-register.csv`. *Test* is the name the runner reported, as the chapter cites it, and *What it checks* the test's own description where it has one. *Added in* is the commit that introduced the test and *Phase* that commit's phase (H: the hardening of Phases 1 to 6). Every result is from the campaign's commit; under each suite is the CI run that also ran it, where CI runs that suite.
